def binary_c5(h_bits, n):
    dx = 2.0/n
    mask = (1<<n)-1
    maxc=0
    # positive lags d=0..n-1: count h_i=1,h_{i-d}=0 for i=d..n-1
    # bits: h >> d has bit i = h_{i-d} for i>=d; for i<d zero.
    # Count = (h & (~ (h >> d)) & mask).bit_count()
    # But for d=0 gives 0.
    # negative lags d<0: pairs i-j = -d => h_i=1, h_{i+d}=0? Count = h & ~(h << d?) Let's derive.
For d positive: count_d = # i such that i-d ≥0, h_i=1, h_{i-d}=0. That's i range d..n-1. A = h >> d. bit i of A = h_{i-d} for i≥d, else 0. Count = (h & ~A & mask).bit_count(). For i<d, h_i could be 1, but no pair because i-d<0? In full correlate, pairs with i-j = d require both i,j in range; if i<d no j. A bit i=0, h_i=1 would count false because ~0=1; bad. Need mask for valid i≥d. valid = mask ^ ((1<<d)-1) (bits d..n-1). count = (h & ~A & valid).bit_count(). Or equivalently ((h >> d)???): Count positions j where h_{j+d}=1 and h_j=0: j=0..n-1-d. Let base = h & ((1<<(n-d))-1) (j bits), shifted = (h >> d)?? bit j = h_{j+d}? Actually (h >> d) bit j = h_{j+d}. So count = ((h >> d) & ~h & ((1<<(n-d))-1)).bit_count(). This is easier. For d=0, (h & ~h)=0.
For negative lags d=-p (p>0): pairs i-j=-p => i+p=j, h_i=1, h_{i+p}=0. Count i=0..n-1-p: h_i=1 and h_{i+p}=0. Base h_i bits 0..n-1-p; shifted h_{i+p} bits at i = (h >> p) bit i. Count = (h & ~(h >> p) & ((1<<(n-p))-1)).bit_count().
So loop p=0..n-1:
 pos_count = ((h >> p) & ~h & mask_p).bit_count() where mask_p=(1<<(n-p))-1, p=0 count0.

Simulated annealing with bitset recompute likely enough. Need accelerate C computation. Let's implement binary_cmax(h_bits,n,masks):
```
def binary_cmax(bits, n, masks):
    maxc=0
    h=bits
    # p=0 count 0
    for p in range(1,n):
        mask=masks[p]
        # positive: h shifted by p at lower bits (i+p) & ~h
        c1 = ((h >> p) & ~h & mask).bit_count()
        if c1 > maxc: maxc=c1
        c2 = (h & ~(h >> p) & mask).bit_count()
        if c2 > maxc: maxc=c2
    return maxc
```
This is 2*(n-1) big int ops + bit_count. For n=512, 1022 iterations. Python loop 1000 per eval. 100k evals =100M loop iterations maybe slow (100M Python ~5s? Good. 1M=50s. Feasible.

Let's derive block generation composition exactly:
```
def random_composition(total, parts, rng):
    if parts <=1: return [total]
    cuts = sorted(rng.choice(total-1, parts-1, replace=False))
    lens=[]; prev=0
    for c in cuts:
       lens.append(c-prev); prev=c
    lens.append(total-prev)
    return lens
```
Need total positive; if parts>total impossible. Choose parts ≤ total/2 maybe. For n=250, half=125, parts k max 125. For mean run L, total runs m≈n/L, one-runs k=m//2. Could also use block boundary moves rather than random bit swaps. In bit representation, a boundary move is swapping a bit at boundary (1 adjacent 0). This is deterministic and preserves run structure. It may locally optimize block lengths. But cannot merge/split blocks. We can also allow random global swaps to change structure.

Implement `boundary_moves`:
- Find adjacent pairs i,i+1 with state[i]!=state[i+1]. For each, swap them (move boundary one step). Evaluate. Accept if improves current (greatest descent maybe). Repeat until no improvement or time. This is O(#boundaries*eval). #boundaries ~ n/L ~ 10-30. Very efficient. But with limit maybe.
- After no boundary improvement, random swaps to create new blocks.

However moving a boundary by swapping adjacent bits only shifts boundary by 1? Example bits index: ...1 0... Swap to 0 1: the 1 moves right into zero, zero moves left; the block of ones loses left? def fft_convolve_real(a,b):
```
    na,nb=len(a),len(b)
    nfft=1
    L=na+nb-1
    while nfft<L: nfft <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)[:L]
```

We need provide gradient for objective to SLSQP. Let's implement objective value:
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha):
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full') * (2.0/n)
    m = c.max()
    shifted = alpha*(c-m)
    # stabilize
    maxs = shifted.max() # 0
    s = np.exp(shifted - maxs)
    total = s.sum()
    if total <= 0: total=1.0
    return float(m + (maxs - np.log(total))/alpha) # since maxs=0
```
Actually standard LSE: m + logsumexp(shifted)/α. shifted max=0, logsumexp = log(sum exp(shifted)). maxs=0. If we subtract maxs, logsumshifted = maxs + log(sum), which = logsum. Since maxs=0. So return m + log(sum)/α. But if no max? Use:
```
maxs = shifted.max()
vals = np.exp(shifted - maxs)
return float(m + (maxs + np.log(vals.sum()))/alpha)
```
Since m+c included. Good.
If alpha is small? alpha positive. For alpha maybe 1, okay.

Gradient:
```
def smooth_jac(x,n,alpha):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=...
    m=c.max()
    shifted=alpha*(c-m)
    maxs=shifted.max()
    w=np.exp(shifted-maxs)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: w/=s
    A = np.convolve(1.0-x, w, mode='full')
    start=n-1; A=A[start:start+n]
    B = np.correlate(w, x, mode='full')
    B = B[start::-1]  # length n? If start=n-1, B[n-1::-1] length n.
    return dx*(A-B)
```
Need ensure B length enough. For w length 2n-1, x n, correlate full length 3n-2, indices 0..3n-3. B[n-1::-1] from n-1 to 0 length n. Good.
Check with n=1? n even ≥? For n=2 works. For n=1 start0, B[0::-1] length1. A slice [0:1]. Good.

But is gradient with weights w normalized over c including dx? c includes dx. Derivative of F=∑w c; w independent of x? Wait weights depend on x. In our derivative F=LSE = ∑ w(x)c(x). But ∂F = ∑ w ∂c (since ∑ w=1 and derivative of w times c? Actually LSE derivative property: ∂ LSE = ∑ e^{α(c-m)}/S ∂c, no extra terms from m/weights. Because F = ∑ w c with w softmax, derivative includes derivative of w but cancels due weighted expectation? Yes F=∑w c, ∂=∑∂w c +∑w∂c. ∑∂w c = ∑(w(α(∂c - ∑w ∂c))) c? = α(∑w∂c*∑wc -∑wc)=0 since ∑wc=F? More directly logsumexp derivative = softmax·∂c. So formula correct with normalized w.

```
def project_sum(x,n):
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    # Use bisection on x + t
    low=-1.0; high=1.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=(low+high)/2
       val=np.sum(np.clip(x+mid,0,1))
       if val < target: low=mid
       else: high=mid
    return np.clip(x+(low+high)/2,0,1)
```
Does x + t projection preserve approximate? It finds smallest t such that sum= target? Because sum(clip(x+t)) continuous nondecreasing. If t can be negative/positive. Bounds low=-1, high=1 enough since x∈[0,1] target∈[0,n]. If original x clipped. Good. But if target exactly achievable? yes. However adding same t to all may not be the projection? It is a monotone recentering; fine. It may not minimize distance but feasible. For final exact sum, we can also adjust residual exactly? Bisection 80 gives sum error ~n*eps? To ensure exact for evaluate? Target task says sum exactly n/2 equivalently. The evaluator maybe checks? We can enforce exact in floating by after bisection do small correction on non-bound entries: diff=target-sum; if abs diff >1e-15, add diff/(n? to interior). Implement:
```
def make_exact_sum(h,n):
   h = project_sum(h,n); diff = n/2 - h.sum();
   if abs(diff)>1e-15:
      if diff>0:
         idx = np.where(h < 1 - 1e-12)[0]
         if len(idx): h[idx] += diff/len(idx)
      else:
         idx = np.where(h > 1e-12)[0]
         if len(idx): h[idx] += diff/len(idx)
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```

Implement `binary_cmax_limit` optimized:
```
def binary_cmax_limit(bits,n,masks,limit):
    maxc=0
    h=bits
    # optional quick if limit <0 return limit+1
    for p in range(1,n):
        mask=masks[p]
        c = ((h >> p) & ~h & mask).bit_count()
        if c > maxc:
            if c > limit:
                return c
            maxc=c
        c = (h & ~(h >> p) & mask).bit_count()
        if c > maxc:
            if c > limit:
                return c
            maxc=c
    return maxc
```
Note if candidate exact ≤limit, returns maxc. If c > limit, returns c (maybe >limit). For acceptance eval with limit = current+T or best? We can decide.

But `~h` in Python negative infinite bits; `& mask` after okay but order: `((h >> p) & ~h & mask)` works because & left assoc: ((h>>p)&~h)&mask. `~h` has ones beyond n, but final mask. Good. Need mask for p: bits 0..n-p-1. For p=0 not used. Precompute masks list length n: masks=[0]*n; for p in 1..n-1: masks[p]=(1<<(n-p))-1. For n=1000, big ints size okay.

```
def sample_move(state, ones, zeros, rng):

    oi = ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]
    return zi, oi
```

```
def random_block_bits(n, mean_run, start, rng):
   half=n//2
   # target total runs even m
   if mean_run <=0: mean_run=1
   m = int(round(n / max(1.0, mean_run)))
   if m < 2: m=2
   if m %2: m +=1
   # cap m to 2*(half-1)? if m/2 > half-1? compose into at most half? one-runs k can be half (all length1) if m=2half, m=n? For n=500, m max500. composition total half=250 into k=250 ok.
   if m > 2*half: m=2*half
   k=m//2
   len1 = random_composition(half,k,rng)
   len0 = random_composition(half,k,rng)
   # Interleave: if start1: [len1[0],len0[0],len1[1],len0[1]...]
   bits=0; pos=0
   for i in range(k):
       if start==1:
          o=len1[i]; z=len0[i]
       else:
          o=len0[i]; z=len1[i]
       if o: bits |= ((1<<o)-1)<<pos; pos+=o
       # zero run no bits
       if z: pos+=z
   return bits
```

Maybe use pair search continuous limited: Use gradient to identify high positive and negative entries, try transferring mass from high to low with small delta. We can compute P = smooth_grad?
```
def gradient_pair_polish(h,n,deadline,alpha=10000):
   h=project(h,n); best=exact; best_h=h.copy()
   for it in range(2000):
      if time>deadline: break
      g=smooth_jac(h,n,alpha)
      # we want h - lr*g. Positive g means increase objective by increasing h? Wait gradient ascent; to reduce, move h_i down if g_i>0, up if g_i<0.
      # Choose i with largest positive g, j with smallest (most negative) g.
      pos = np.argsort(g) # ascending
      j = pos[0] (g neg), i = pos[-1] (g pos)
      if i==j or h[i]<=0 or h[j]>=1: try next top/bottom candidates.
      delta = min(h[i], 1-h[j], 0.02) maybe.
      newh=h.copy(); newh[i]-=delta; newh[j]+=delta
      newh=project(newh,n)
      c=exact; if c<best: update
      else try smaller delta 0.005, or random pair.
   return best_h,best
```

Choosing decrease high g positive and increase low g negative: d_i<0, d_j>0; product g_i(-δ)+g_j(+δ)=δ(g_j-g_i)<0. Good.

`project_sum(x,n)`:
```
def project_sum(x,n):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x,0.0,1.0,out=x)
    target=n/2.0
    s=x.sum()
    if abs(s-target) <= 1e-14:
       return x
    low=-1.0; high=1.0
    # ensure low gives sum <= target, high >= target
    for _ in range(60):
        mid=(low+high)*0.5
        v=x+mid
        np.clip(v,0.0,1.0,out=v)
        if v.sum() < target:
            low=mid
        else:
            high=mid
    x += (low+high)*0.5
    np.clip(x,0.0,1.0,out=x)
    return x
```
Is x+mid clip in-place: if v alias x? We can't `np.clip(x+mid,...out=x)` because x overwrites. Need temp. 60*n for n=500 small. Could do:
```
tmp = x + mid
np.clip(tmp,0,1,out=tmp)
s=tmp.sum()
```

`make_feasible` adjust exact residual:
```
def make_feasible(x,n):
    h=project_sum(x,n)
    diff=n/2.0 - h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(h < 1.0 - 1e-9)[0]
       else:
          idx=np.where(h > 1e-9)[0]
       if idx.size:
          # distribute only small fraction avoiding bound
          step = diff / idx.size
          # limit step to not violate bounds
          if diff>0:
             lim = np.min(1.0 - h[idx]) - 1e-12
             if step > lim: step=lim
          else:
             lim = np.min(h[idx]) - 1e-12
             if step < -lim: step=-lim
          h[idx] += step
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    # one more exact at last feasible
    diff=n/2.0-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       if diff>0:
          idx=np.where(h<1.0)[0]
       else:
          idx=np.where(h>0.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          h[idx[-1]] += diff  # may violate? choose interior
          # if violated, distribute?
    return h
```
This residual adjustment may push last beyond bounds if diff too large. Better distribute evenly over all interior; as above. We can do iterative few times:
```
for _ in range(5):
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
 if diff>0:
   idx=np.nonzero(h<1-1e-12)[0]
 else: idx=np.nonzero(h>1e-12)[0]
 if idx.size==0: break
 add=diff/idx.size
 if diff>0:
    maxadd=np.min(1-h[idx])
    if add>maxadd: add=maxadd*0.5
 else:
    maxadd=np.min(h[idx])
    if add<-maxadd: add=-maxadd*0.5
 h[idx]+=add
```
Good.

`exact_c5`:

```
def exact_c5(h,n):
```

`smooth_value`:
```
def smooth_value(x,n,alpha):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')*dx
    m=float(c.max())
    shifted=alpha*(c-m)
    # shifted <=0, max 0. Use np.max for stability
    sm=shifted.max()
    vals=np.exp(shifted-sm)
    s=vals.sum()
    if s<=0:
       s=1.0
    return float(m + (sm + math.log(s))/alpha)
```
If alpha very small? okay. If alpha large and all shifted -10000, vals max=exp(0)=1, s>=1. Good.

`smooth_jac` as above. Need if alpha large causing overflow? shifted-sm <=0.
```
def smooth_jac(x,n,alpha):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')*dx
    m=float(c.max())
    shifted=alpha*(c-m)
    sm=shifted.max()
    w=np.exp(shifted-sm)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0
    else:
       w/=s
    v=1.0-x
    A=np.convolve(v,w,mode='full')
    start=n-1
    A=A[start:start+n]
    B=np.correlate(w,x,mode='full')
    B=B[start::-1]
    return dx*(A-B)
```
Potential if n large and start+ n > len(A)? A len = n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. start+n=2n-1 <=3n-2 for n≥1. OK. B len same; start= n-1 down to 0. OK.
But note `np.correlate(w,x,mode='full')` with w longer than x; definition? We need correlate(w,x) exactly. Yes.
Could use direct; for n=500 okay.

```
def slsqp_optimize(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    x0=make_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con_fun,'jac':sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_value, x0, args=(n,alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'disp':False})
       if res.x is not None:
          return make_feasible(res.x,n)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return x0
```

`make_masks_binary(n)`:
```
def make_masks_binary(n):
    masks=[0]*n
    for p in range(1,n):
        masks[p]=(1<<(n-p))-1
    return masks
```

```
def make_bit_masks(n): return [1<<i for i in range(n)]
```

`random_block_bits(n,mean_run,start,rng)`:
```
def random_composition(total, parts, rng):
    if parts <= 1:
        return [total]
    if parts > total:
        parts = total
    # choose parts-1 distinct cuts from 1..total-1
    if parts == total:
        return [1]*total
    cuts = [int(c) for c in rng.choice(total-1, parts-1, replace=False)]
    cuts.sort()
    comp=[]; prev=0
    for cc in cuts:
       if cc>prev: comp.append(cc-prev); prev=cc
       else: comp.append(1); prev=cc # shouldn't
    comp.append(total-prev)
    return comp
```
But if total-1=0 (total=1) and parts>1 impossible; handle.
```
def random_block_bits(n, mean_run, start, rng):
    half=n//2
    if half <=0: return 0
    if mean_run < 1: mean_run=1
    m = int(round(float(n)/float(mean_run)))
    if m < 2: m=2
    if m % 2 == 1: m +=1
    max_m = 2*half
    if m > max_m: m = max_m
    k = m//2
    len1 = random_composition(half,k,rng)
    len0 = random_composition(half,k,rng)
    bits=0; pos=0
    for i in range(k):
       if start:
          o=len1[i]; z=len0[i]
       else:
          o=len0[i]; z=len1[i]
       if o>0:
          bits |= ((1<<o)-1) << pos
          pos += o
       pos += z
    return bits
```
If k parts > half? cap. For n=250, half=125, mean_run=2 -> m=125 even? if odd+1=126, k=63 okay. mean_run=1 -> m=250, k=125, composition half into 125 parts returns all ones. Good.

`continuous_to_bits(arr,n)`:
```
def continuous_to_bits(arr,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(arr,float),0,1)
    target=n//2
    if target<=0: return 0
    if target>=n: return (1<<n)-1
    # top target indices
    idx=np.argpartition(h, n-target)[:target]
    bits=0
    for i in idx: bits |= 1<<int(i)
    return bits
```
If duplicates, argpartition picks arbitrary. Good.

`bits_to_array`:
```
def bits_to_array(bits,n):
    arr=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
    for i in range(n): arr[i]=1.0 if (bits>>i)&1 else 0.0
    return arr
```
For n=500 fine. Could vectorize using bytes but okay.

`binary_search(n,start_bits_list,deadline,rng)`:
- Build masks, bitmasks once.
- Evaluate start bits: `c=binary_cmax_limit(bits,n,masks,10**9)`. Keep list (bits,c).
- Sort by c ascending.
- If time enough, refine top K. Need K based on time. If len starts maybe 18. Set per_refine = max(5, min(30, (deadline-time)/topK)). topK maybe 6 for 400s. But if starts bad, more.
```
def binary_search(n, start_bits, deadline, rng):
    masks=make_masks_binary(n); bit_masks=make_bit_masks(n)
    scored=[]
    for bits in start_bits:
       if bits is None: continue
       # ensure bits length no overflow; maybe mask full
       c=binary_cmax_limit(bits,n,masks,10**9)
       scored.append((c,bits))
    scored.sort()
    best_bits=scored[0][1] if scored else 0; best_c=scored[0][0] if scored else large
    # allocate remaining
    if not scored: return None, inf
    # greedily refine candidates: for each cand in scored:
    for idx,(c0,bits) in enumerate(scored):
       if time.time() > deadline - 5: break
       remaining=deadline-time.time()
       # allocate based on rank and remaining starts
       # If idx==0 (best) allocate maybe 2/3 remaining? But we want explore.
       # Use min(60 for first, 20 for next ...)
       if idx==0:
           alloc=min(120, remaining*0.5)
       elif idx==1:
           alloc=min(60, remaining*0.35)
       else:
           alloc=min(20, remaining*0.15)
       if alloc < 5: break
       deadline_i=time.time()+alloc
       b,bc=refine_binary(bits,n,masks,bit_masks,rng,deadline_i,T0=6.0,Tend=0.05)
       if bc < best_c:
           best_c=bc; best_bits=b
       # after refining first, maybe if best improves, subsequent starts less promising? use c0 as lower? no.
    return best_bits,best_c
```
But if best initial c0 and refine uses up too much, subsequent starts not tried. We can cap first to e.g. 80s. Since 400s total, top1 100, top2 50, top3 30, top4 20, top5 10, etc. Use loop.

```
def parent_bits_for_n(parent,n):
   if parent is None: return None
   if len(parent)==n:
       arr=parent
   else:
       x_old=np.linspace(0,2,len(parent)); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n)
       arr=np.interp(x_new,x_old,parent)
   arr=make_feasible(arr,n)
   return continuous_to_bits(arr,n)
```

`gradient_polish` implementation details:
```
def gradient_polish(h,n,rng,deadline):
    h=make_feasible(h,n); best=exact_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    alpha=20000
    no_improve_time=time.time()
    step_counts=[100,50,20]
    while time.time() < deadline:
        g=smooth_jac(h,n,alpha)
        # candidate sources: top 30 positive, sinks top 30 negative
        pos_idx=np.where(g>0)[0]
        neg_idx=np.where(g<0)[0]
        if pos_idx.size==0 or neg_idx.size==0:
            alpha=max(100,alpha*0.5); continue
        # sort? argsort full O(n log n); okay.
        order=np.argsort(g)
        neg_idx=order[:min(30,n)]
        pos_idx=order[-min(30,n):][::-1]
        improved=False
        t_iteration=time.time()
        for _ in range(30): # candidate count
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            i=int(pos_idx[rng.randint(len(pos_idx))])
            j=int(neg_idx[rng.randint(len(neg_idx))])
            if i==j: continue
            # try delta fractions of h[i],1-h[j]
            md=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
            if md <= 1e-6: continue
            for frac in [0.5,0.2,0.05,0.01]:
                d=md*frac
                nh=h.copy(); nh[i]-=d; nh[j]+=d; nh=make_feasible(nh,n)
                c=exact_c5(nh,n)
                if c < best - 1e-15:
                    h=nh; best=c; best_h=nh.copy(); improved=True; no_improve_time=time.time(); break
            if time.time() - t_iteration > 5: break
        if not improved:
            # occasionally allow random uphill based on best? or lower alpha
            if time.time() - no_improve_time > 5:
                # reset to best? if h != best maybe
                h=best_h.copy(); no_improve_time=time.time()
                alpha *= 0.7
                if alpha < 50: break
    return best_h,best
```

Run representation: `runs` list of lengths, start_bit (1 if first run ones). Alternating. Total length n, total ones sum over indices parity. Move types:
- Define boundaries between runs i and i+1. A transfer of 1 unit from run i to run i+1 (runs[i]-=1, runs[i+1]+=1 if runs[i]>1) preserves total length. If run i is 1-run, total ones changes by -1; if run i is 0-run, +1. To preserve total ones, do two transfers of opposite sign: one transfer from a 1-run to 0-run (decrease ones) and one transfer from a 0-run to 1-run (increase ones). Equivalently move one unit from a 1-run at boundary A to the adjacent 0-run, and from a 0-run at boundary B to adjacent 1-run. This shifts boundaries in a way. We can choose first index i where runs[i] is 1-run and runs[i]>1, second index j where runs[j] is 0-run and runs[j]>1; apply runs[i]-=1, runs[i+1]+=1, runs[j]-=1, runs[j+1]+=1 (if i and j not adjacent causing merge? Need handle if adjacent pairs overlap). If i and j adjacent or same, could alter total? Choose distinct non-overlapping; if length drops to 0 and adjacent same type, runs merge. We can normalize.
- Also reverse direction? The above reduces a 1-run and a 0-run, increases adjacent opposite. Actually moving mass from 1-run to its zero neighbor and from zero-run to its one neighbor: total ones constant. This can shift boundaries. We can also transfer opposite: increase 1-run and decrease zero-run paired reverse; but choosing i/j as described plus reverse is just choose different boundaries.
- More general: choose any two boundaries; if orientation same, two transfers of same sign change total ones by ±2, not allowed. If opposite, allowed. We can implement.

Function `runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)`:
```
def runs_to_bits(runs,start,n):
   bits=0; pos=0; cur=start
   for L in runs:
      if cur==1 and L>0:
         bits |= ((1<<L)-1) << pos
      pos += L
      cur ^=1
   return bits & ((1<<n)-1)
```

Compress bits to runs:
```
def bits_to_runs(bits,n):
   runs=[]; cur=(bits&1); start=cur; count=0
   for i in range(n):
      bit=(bits>>i)&1
      if bit==cur: count+=1
      else: runs.append(count); cur=bit; count=1
   runs.append(count)
   return runs,start
```

Generating block start:
```
def random_block_runs(n, mean_run, start, rng):
    half=n//2
    k=...
    len1=random_composition(half,k,rng)
    len0=random_composition(half,k,rng)
    runs=[]
    if start==1:
       for i in range(k): runs.extend([len1[i],len0[i]])
    else:
       for i in range(k): runs.extend([len0[i],len1[i]])
    return runs,start
```

Block refine implementation more detailed:
```
def block_is_one(runs,start,i):
   if start==1: return i%2==0
   return i%2==1

def block_candidates(runs):
```

Simpler:
```
def is_one_run(i,start):
    if start==1:
       return (i % 2 == 0)
    else:
       return (i % 2 == 1)
```
In block refine:

```
def random_composition(total, parts, min_len, rng):
   if parts <=1: return [total] if total>=min_len else [0?]
   if min_len <1: min_len=1
   if parts*min_len > total:
      parts = max(1,total//min_len)
   base=[min_len]*parts
   rem=total-parts*min_len
   if rem>0:
      add=rng.multinomial(rem, parts)
      base=[b+a for b,a in zip(base,add)]
   # If some zeros? no
   return base
```

`binary_search` modified:
Inputs: n, list of initial `(runs,start,bits?)`, maybe bits. We can compute bits from runs.
```
def binary_search(n, init_runs_starts, deadline, rng):
    masks=make_masks_binary(n)
    scored=[]
    for runs,start in init_runs_starts:
       if len(runs)==0: continue
       bits=runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
       # ensure half? If not, maybe skip or adjust via bits_to_runs? Let's count:
       if bits.bit_count()!=n//2: continue
       c=binary_cmax_limit(bits,n,masks,10**9)
       scored.append((c, runs[:], start))
    if not scored: return None,inf
    scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    best_bits=runs_to_bits(scored[0][1],scored[0][2],n); best_c=scored[0][0]
    # refine top using block_refine with allocations
    # We need bit_masks for optional bit_refine? maybe not.
    for idx in range(min(len(scored), 6)):
       if time.time()>deadline-5: break
       remaining=deadline-time.time()
       if idx==0: alloc=min(150, remaining*0.45)
       elif idx==1: alloc=min(80, remaining*0.30)
       else: alloc=min(40, remaining*0.15)
       if alloc<5: break
       runs,start=scored[idx][1],scored[idx][2]
       # block_refine modifies, returns best_bits,c
       dead_i=time.time()+alloc
       b,bc,br,bs=block_refine(runs,start,n,masks,rng,dead_i)
       if bc<best_c:
          best_c=bc; best_bits=b
       # optional bit_refine if time left after this? For top improved maybe
    return best_bits,best_c
```

```
def is_one_run(i,start):
    if start == 1:
        return (i % 2 == 0)
    else:
        return (i % 2 == 1)
```

```
def block_best_improve(runs,start,n,masks,current,time_limit):
    s1=[]; s0=[]
    for i in range(len(runs)-1):
        if runs[i] > 1:
            if is_one_run(i,start): s1.append(i)
            else: s0.append(i)
    if not s1 or not s0: return False
    best_c=10**9; best_move=None
    for a in s1:
        for b in s0:
            # if time limit and best found? still maybe continue until limit
            runs[a]-=1; runs[a+1]+=1; runs[b]-=1; runs[b+1]+=1
            nb=runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
            c=binary_cmax_limit(nb,n,masks,max(0,current-1))
            if c < best_c:
                best_c=c; best_move=(a,b)
            runs[a]+=1; runs[a+1]-=1; runs[b]+=1; runs[b+1]-=1
            if time.time() > time_limit:
                break
        if time.time() > time_limit: break
    if best_move is not None:
        a,b=best_move
        runs[a]-=1; runs[a+1]+=1; runs[b]-=1; runs[b+1]+=1
        return True
    return False
```

```
def generate_starts(n,parent,bits_parent,rng):
   starts=[]
   if bits_parent is not None and bits_parent.bit_count()==n//2:
       runs,start=bits_to_runs(bits_parent,n)
       starts.append((runs,start))
   props = [0.02,0.04,0.07,0.10,0.13,0.18,0.25,0.35]
   rng.shuffle? props fixed.
   for pr in props:
       mean=max(2,int(round(n*pr)))
       min_len = 2 if mean >= 6 else 1
       for st in (0,1):
          runs,start=random_block_runs(n,mean,st,min_len,rng)
          starts.append((runs,start))
   return starts
```

Need `random_block_runs` uses `random_composition` with min_len. Ensure total half. `k` computation with mean_run: target total runs m=n/mean. For min_len 2, if k reduced by composition? We'll compute k then compositions may reduce if total parts*min_len>half. That increases average run. Good.
```
def random_block_runs(n, mean_run, start, min_len, rng):
   half=n//2
   m = max(2, int(round(n / max(1.0, mean_run))))
   if m%2: m+=1
   if m > 2*half: m=2*half
   k=m//2
   len1 = random_composition(half,k,min_len,rng)
   len0 = random_composition(half,k,min_len,rng)
   # If due min_len, lengths of len1 maybe fewer? random_composition may return fewer than k if parts reduced. Need use actual lengths; interleave min(len)
   L1=len(len1); L0=len(len0); m_act=min(L1,L0)
   runs=[]
   if start==1:
      for i in range(m_act): runs.extend([len1[i], len0[i]])
   else:
      for i in range(m_act): runs.extend([len0[i], len1[i]])
   return runs,start
```
If composition returns total parts maybe less than k, total ones still half? random_composition returns list length actual parts, sums half. Interleaving min lengths uses all (L1=L0 if same k). Good. If one reduced, both same k input so same actual. Good.

Now details for `optimize_continuous`: We need call SLSQP multiple times. If SLSQP fails, h unchanged. It may be slow if maxiter high. Use `maxiter` based on n. Let's set:
```
def optimize_continuous(h0,n,deadline):
    h=make_feasible(h0,n)
    best_c=exact_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    if n <= 300:
       passes=[(100,160),(800,200),(3000,200),(10000,160)]
    elif n <= 600:
       passes=[(100,100),(800,120),(3000,120),(10000,100)]
    else:
       passes=[(200,60),(1000,80),(5000,80)]
    for a,mi in passes:
       if time.time() >= deadline-2: break
       # If previous h not improving, still run? yes
       h2=slsqp_optimize(h,n,a,mi)
       c2=exact_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
          best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2
       # after improvement, maybe if time left, continue; if no improvement, continue
    return best_h,best_c
```
This can exceed deadline because SLSQP maxiter may take unknown. But maxiter capped. For n=400, maxiter 120*4=480 iterations; SLSQP each maybe 0.02s ->10s. safe. For n=500 maybe okay. If it takes 1s per iter, 480s too slow. Need reduce. What is SLSQP speed? Hard. We can further reduce maxiter: n>=400: passes 3 with 80 total 240. Maybe safe. Let's estimate SLSQP objective+gradient: For n=400, c correlate direct np.correlate full O(n^2) C maybe 0.0002s? conv/correlate in jac another 0.0005, total 0.001; SLSQP each iter maybe 5-10 calls ->0.01-0.05s. 240 -> 2-12s. Fine. n=500 -> maybe 0.02s ->5s. Good.

```
def gradient_polish(h,n,rng,deadline):
    h=make_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=exact_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    alpha=20000.0
    no_imp=time.time()
    while time.time() < deadline:
        g=smooth_jac(h,n,alpha)
        order=np.argsort(g)
        ntest=min(20,n)
        neg_idx=order[:ntest]
        pos_idx=order[n-n:test?][::-1]  Need slice
```
Avoid lambda; slicing:
```
pos_idx = order[n - ntest:][::-1]
```
If ntest>n, ntest=n, order[0:][::-1]. Good.
```
        improved=False
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time()>deadline: break
            i=int(pos_idx[rng.randint(len(pos_idx))])
            j=int(neg_idx[rng.randint(len(neg_idx))])
            if i==j: continue
            md=min(h[i], 1.0-h[j])
            if md <= 1e-8: continue
            for frac in [0.5,0.2,0.05,0.01]:
                 d=md*frac
                 nh=h.copy(); nh[i]-=d; nh[j]+=d; nh=make_feasible(nh,n)
                 c=exact_c5(nh,n)
                 if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                     h=nh; best_c=c; best_h=nh.copy(); improved=True; no_imp=time.time(); break
                 if time.time()>deadline: break
        if not improved:
            if time.time()-no_imp > 6:
                h=best_h.copy(); no_imp=time.time(); alpha *= 0.6
                if alpha < 100: break
    return best_h,best_c
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # parent
    parent=None; nparent=0
    try:
       p=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if p.size>0:
          nparent=p.size
          p=make_feasible(p,nparent)
          parent_c=exact_c5(p,nparent)
          parent=p
    except Exception:
       parent=None; parent_c=inf
    best_h=None; best_c=inf; best_n=0
    if parent is not None:
       best_h=parent.copy(); best_c=parent_c; best_n=nparent
    else:
       n=250
       bits=random_block_bits? We have random_block_runs. Use random_block_runs(n,30,1,2,rng); bits=runs_to_bits...
       best_h=bits_to_array(bits,n); best_c=exact_c5(best_h,n); best_n=n
    # time left
    # Phase quick continuous for parent if available and not too expensive
    if parent is not None and time.time() < deadline - 60:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       alloc=min(80.0, rem*0.12)
       if alloc >= 15:
          hopt,copt=optimize_continuous(parent,nparent,time.time()+alloc)
          if copt < best_c:
             best_h=hopt; best_c=copt; best_n=nparent
    # Binary search
    if time.time() < deadline - 40:
       # choose n_list
       n_set=[]
       if parent is not None and nparent % 2 == 0 and 128 <= nparent <= 320:
          n_set.append(nparent)
       n_set.append(400)
       # maybe if time big, n_set.append(512)? We can adapt.
       # use 2 n's to not overdo. If nparent==400 avoid dup.
       unique=[]
       for nn in n_set:
          if nn not in unique: unique.append(nn)
       # remove duplicates: simple loop
       unique_n=[]
       for nn in n_set:
          if nn not in unique_n: unique_n.append(nn)
       binary_deadline = time.time() + min(400.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.65)
       # allocate per n proportionally by n?
       for idx,nn in enumerate(unique_n):
          if time.time() > binary_deadline - 20: break
          remaining_to_binary=binary_deadline-time.time()
          # alloc fraction: if two n, first 0.5, second 0.5 adjusted by n
          if len(unique_n)==1: alloc=remaining_to_binary
          else:
             # earlier n maybe lower weight; parent n lower res but faster; high n more. Use equal maybe.
             alloc=remaining_to_binary*0.5
          # cap high n to 220? 
          if nn > 350: alloc=min(alloc, 220.0)
          else: alloc=min(alloc, 150.0)
          alloc=max(10.0, min(alloc, remaining_to_binary))
          if time.time()+alloc < deadline - 30:
             starts=generate_block_starts(nn,parent,rng)
             b,bc=binary_search(nn,starts,time.time()+alloc,rng)
             if b is not None and bc < best_c*? bc int. Convert
                hbin=bits_to_array(b,nn)
                cbound=bc*(2.0/nn)
                cbound=exact_c5(hbin,nn) # verify
                if cbound < best_c:
                   best_h=hbin; best_c=cbound; best_n=nn
    # Final continuous optimization on current best
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 25:
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       # first optimize exact best
       alloc1=min(rem*0.5, 180.0)
       if alloc1 > 20:
          hopt,copt=optimize_continuous(best_h,best_n,time.time()+alloc1)
          if copt<best_c: update
       # noise restarts if time left
       scales=[0.02,0.05,0.1]
       for sc in scales:
          if time.time() > deadline - 25: break
          rem=deadline-time.time()
          alloc=min(rem*0.35, 80.0)
          if alloc < 15: break
          hnoise=best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sc, best_n)
          hopt,copt=optimize_continuous(hnoise,best_n,time.time()+alloc)
          if copt<best_c: update
    # gradient polish
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 8:
       hp,cp=gradient_polish(best_h,best_n,rng,deadline-2)
       if cp<best_c: update
    # final project and exact
    if best_h is None: fallback...
    best_h=make_feasible(best_h,best_n)
    best_c=exact_c5(best_h,best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

```
def parent_bits_for_n(parent,n):
   if parent is None: return None
   if len(parent)==n:
      arr=parent
   else:
      xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,len(parent))
      xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
      arr=np.interp(xn,xo,parent)
   arr=make_feasible(arr,n)
   return continuous_to_bits(arr,n)
```

```
def generate_block_starts(n,parent,rng):

    if bp is not None and bp.bit_count()==n//2:

    props=[0.02,0.04,0.06,0.09,0.12,0.16,0.22,0.30]
```

```
def run_source_lists(runs,start,max_each=40):
   s1=[]; s0=[]
   for i in range(len(runs)-1):
      if runs[i] > 1:
         if is_one_run(i,start): s1.append(i)
         else: s0.append(i)
   if len(s1)>max_each:
      step=max(1, len(s1)//max_each)
      s1=s1[::step]
   if len(s0)>max_each:
      step=max(1, len(s0)//max_each)
      s0=s0[::step]
   return s1,s0
```

`random_composition`:
```
def random_composition(total, parts, min_len, rng):
    if total <= 0:
        return []
    if min_len < 1: min_len=1
    if parts < 1: parts=1
    if parts * min_len > total:
        parts = total // min_len
        if parts <= 0:
            return [total]
    if parts == 1:
        return [total]
    base = [min_len] * parts
    rem = total - parts * min_len
    if rem > 0:
        add = rng.multinomial(rem, parts)
        for i in range(parts):
            base[i] += int(add[i])
    return base
```
If total=1,min_len=1, parts after cap maybe1, returns [1]. Good. If parts*min_len > total and total//min_len=0 only if total<min_len; for total>0 min_len could >total (e.g., min_len=3,total=2). Then parts=0, return [total] violates min_len but ensures sum. Good.

Detailed function code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project_sum(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    if abs(float(x.sum()) - target) <= 1e-14:
        return x
    low = -1.0
    high = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        tmp = x + mid
        np.clip(tmp, 0.0, 1.0, out=tmp)
        s = float(tmp.sum())
        if s < target:
            low = mid
        else:
            high = mid
    x += 0.5 * (low + high)
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    return x
```

`make_feasible`:
```
def make_feasible(x, n):
    h = project_sum(x, n)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.nonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            add = diff / float(idx.size)
            max_add = float(np.min(1.0 - h[idx]))
            if add > max_add:
                add = 0.5 * max_add
            if add <= 0: break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = np.nonzero(h > 1e-12)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            add = diff / float(idx.size)
            max_add = float(np.min(h[idx]))
            if add < -max_add:
                add = -0.5 * max_add
            if add >= 0: break
            h[idx] += add
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h
```
If idx.size=1 and diff large, add limited to max_add, may not fully; loop repeats. Good.

`exact_c5`:
```
def exact_c5(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / float(n))
    return float(np.max(c))
```

`smooth_value` and `smooth_jac` as above. In smooth_jac, guard n==1:
```
if n == 1:
   B = Bfull[0:1]
else:
   B = Bfull[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1]
```
A slice for n=1: start=0, A[0:1]. Good.

`sum_con_jac` returns `np.ones_like(x, dtype=float)` maybe length n. If x is array length n. Good.

`slsqp_optimize`:
```
def slsqp_optimize(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
    x0=make_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] # list comprehension ok? no closure.
```
List comprehension uses `_`; okay. Could loop. Use list comprehension not lambda. Fine.
```
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':sum_con_fun,'jac':sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
    try:
       res = minimize(smooth_value, x0, args=(n, alpha), method='SLSQP', jac=smooth_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-10,'disp':False})
       x = res.x
       if x is not None and not np.any(np.isnan(x)):
          return make_feasible(x,n)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return x0
```

`optimize_continuous`:
```
def optimize_continuous(h0,n,deadline):
    h=make_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    if n <= 300:
       passes = [(50,120),(500,150),(2500,150),(10000,120)]
    elif n <= 600:
       passes = [(50,90),(500,100),(2500,100),(10000,90)]
    else:
       passes = [(100,70),(1000,80),(5000,80)]
    for alpha, mi in passes:
       if time.time() >= deadline - 2.0: break
       h2 = slsqp_optimize(h,n,alpha,mi)
       c2 = exact_c5(h2,n)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
           best_c = c2; best_h = h2.copy(); h = h2
    return best_h, best_c
```
If SLSQP call takes >deadline, can't know. But maxiter caps.

Binary functions:
```
def make_masks_binary(n):
    masks=[0]*n
    for p in range(1,n):
        masks[p] = (1 << (n - p)) - 1
    return masks
```
`binary_cmax_limit`:
```
def binary_cmax_limit(bits, n, masks, limit):
    maxc = 0
    h = bits
    for p in range(1, n):
        mask = masks[p]
        c = ((h >> p) & ~h & mask).bit_count()
        if c > maxc:
            if c > limit:
                return c
            maxc = c
        c = (h & ~(h >> p) & mask).bit_count()
        if c > maxc:
            if c > limit:
                return c
            maxc = c
    return maxc
```
If limit <0 and maxc=0? For current=0, limit max(0,-1)=0; if maxc=0 not >limit, returns 0. Good.

`random_composition` as above. Use `rng.multinomial(rem, parts)` returns numpy array; if `rem` large maybe int; okay. But if parts is large and rem small, multinomial may be okay. If parts=0? handled.

`random_block_runs`:
```
def random_block_runs(n, mean_run, start, min_len, rng):
    half = n // 2
    if half <= 0:
        return [], start
    m = int(round(float(n) / float(max(1, mean_run))))
    if m < 2: m=2
    if m % 2 == 1: m += 1
    if m > 2 * half: m = 2 * half
    k = m // 2
    len1 = random_composition(half, k, min_len, rng)
    len0 = random_composition(half, k, min_len, rng)
    L = min(len(len1), len(len0))
    runs = []
    if start == 1:
       for i in range(L):
          runs.append(len1[i]); runs.append(len0[i])
    else:
       for i in range(L):
          runs.append(len0[i]); runs.append(len1[i])
    return runs, start
```
If start maybe 0/1 int.

`bits_to_runs`:
```
def bits_to_runs(bits,n):
    if n <=0: return [],0
    first = bits & 1
    start = int(first)
    runs=[]
    cur=start
    count=0
    for i in range(n):
        b = (bits >> i) & 1
        if b == cur:
            count +=1
        else:
            runs.append(count)
            cur = b
            count=1
    runs.append(count)
    return runs,start
```
If bits has count not half, okay.

`runs_to_bits`:
```
def runs_to_bits(runs,start,n):
    bits=0
    pos=0
    cur=start
    for L in runs:
        if L > 0:
            if cur == 1:
                bits |= ((1 << L) - 1) << pos
            pos += L
            cur ^= 1
        else:
            # zero length skip but parity? If L=0, don't change cur? If we skip and don't toggle, adjacency? Should not happen.
            pass
    if n >=0:
        bits &= (1 << n) - 1
    return bits
```
If L=0, maybe should not toggle; runs positive from our functions. Good.

`bits_to_array`:
```
def bits_to_array(bits,n):
    arr=np.empty(n,dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
       arr[i] = 1.0 if ((bits >> i) & 1) else 0.0
    return arr
```

`continuous_to_bits`:
```
def continuous_to_bits(arr,n):
    h=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target=n//2
    if target <=0: return 0
    if target >= n: return (1<<n)-1
    kth=n-target
    if kth <0: kth=0
    if kth >= n: kth=n-1
    idx=np.argpartition(h,kth)
    idx=idx[kth:]
    bits=0
    for ii in idx:
        bits |= 1 << int(ii)
    return bits
```
If `h` view of parent and clip modifies? h=np.asarray arr maybe parent array; np.clip out=h could modify parent if no copy. Use `h=np.array(arr,dtype=float).copy()`. Good.

`is_one_run` as above.

`run_source_lists`:
```
def run_source_lists(runs,start,max_each=40):
    s1=[]; s0=[]
    for i in range(len(runs)-1):
        if runs[i] > 1:
            if is_one_run(i,start): s1.append(i)
            else: s0.append(i)
    if len(s1) > max_each:
        step = max(1, len(s1)//max_each)
        s1 = s1[::step]
    if len(s0) > max_each:
        step = max(1, len(s0)//max_each)
        s0 = s0[::step]
    return s1,s0
```

`block_best_improve`:
```
def block_best_improve(runs,start,n,masks,current,time_limit):
    s1,s0 = run_source_lists(runs,start,30)
    if not s1 or not s0:
        return False, None
    best_c = current
    best_move = None
    for a in s1:
        for b in s0:
            # optional if a==b? parities differ so no
            runs[a] -= 1
            runs[a+1] += 1
            runs[b] -= 1
            runs[b+1] += 1
            nb = runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
            c = binary_cmax_limit(nb,n,masks,current-1)
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_move = (a,b)
            runs[a] += 1
            runs[a+1] -= 1
            runs[b] += 1
            runs[b+1] -= 1
            if time.time() > time_limit:
                break
        if time.time() > time_limit:
            break
    if best_move is not None:
        a,b=best_move
        runs[a] -= 1; runs[a+1] += 1; runs[b] -= 1; runs[b+1] += 1
        return True, best_c
    return False, None
```
If `current-1` negative, binary_cmax_limit limit 0 if current=0? current-1=-1, but function checks c>limit when maxc>0; if candidate max 0, returns 0, c<best_c? best_c maybe0 no. Good. But if limit negative and c=0, not >limit? It returns 0. Fine. Could use max(0,current-1). Add:
`lim = current - 1; if lim < 0: lim = 0`.
Use to avoid negative weird.

`block_refine`:
```
def block_refine(runs,start,n,masks,rng,deadline):
    runs=runs[:]
    start=start
    if sum(runs) != n:
        return runs_to_bits(runs,start,n), binary_cmax_limit(...), runs,start? But binary_search ensures.
    bits=runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
    current=binary_cmax_limit(bits,n,masks,1000000000)
    best_c=current; best_bits=bits; best_runs=runs[:]; best_start=start
    no_imp=time.time()
    # deterministic
    det_dead = min(deadline, time.time() + max(5.0, 0.25*(deadline - time.time())))
    # If deadline already less than now? no
    while time.time() < det_dead:
        pass_limit = min(det_dead, time.time() + 3.0)
        ok, nc = block_best_improve(runs,start,n,masks,current,pass_limit)
        if ok:
            current = nc
            bits = runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
            if current < best_c:
                best_c = current; best_bits = bits; best_runs = runs[:]; best_start = start
            no_imp = time.time()
        else:
            break
    # SA
    sa_start = time.time()
    sa_duration = max(1e-6, deadline - sa_start)
    T0=6.0; Tend=0.2
    while time.time() < deadline:
        s1,s0 = run_source_lists(runs,start,60)
        if not s1 or not s0:
            break
        frac = (time.time() - sa_start) / sa_duration
        if frac < 0.0: frac=0.0
        if frac > 1.0: frac=1.0
        T = T0 * (Tend / T0) ** frac
        if T < Tend: T = Tend
        a = s1[int(rng.randint(len(s1)))]
        b = s0[int(rng.randint(len(s0)))]
        runs[a] -= 1; runs[a+1] += 1; runs[b] -= 1; runs[b+1] += 1
        nb = runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
        lim = current + int(math.ceil(T)) + 1
        cand = binary_cmax_limit(nb,n,masks,lim)
        if cand <= lim:
            delta = cand - current
            accept = False
            if delta <= 0:
                accept = True
            else:
                if T > 0:
                    prob = math.exp(-float(delta) / float(T))
                    if rng.random() < prob:
                        accept = True
            if accept:
                current = cand
                bits = nb
                if cand < best_c:
                    best_c = cand; best_bits = bits; best_runs = runs[:]; best_start = start
                no_imp = time.time()
            else:
                runs[a] += 1; runs[a+1] -= 1; runs[b] += 1; runs[b+1] -= 1
        else:
            runs[a] += 1; runs[a+1] -= 1; runs[b] += 1; runs[b+1] -= 1
        if time.time() - no_imp > 10.0 and current > best_c + 4:
            runs = best_runs[:]
            start = best_start
            bits = best_bits
            current = best_c
            no_imp = time.time()
    return best_bits, best_c, best_runs[:], best_start
```
If `cand <= lim` and delta positive but cand may be >lim? no. `cand` exact. If current `best_c` int. Good.

Note `sum(runs)` in `block_refine` uses Python sum; no lambda. Fine.

`generate_block_starts`:
```
def parent_bits_for_n(parent,n):
    if parent is None: return None
    if len(parent) == n:
       arr = np.array(parent, dtype=float).copy()
    else:
       xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(parent))
       xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
       arr = np.interp(xn, xo, parent)
       arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    arr = make_feasible(arr,n)
    return continuous_to_bits(arr,n)
```
If parent length 1? np.interp okay. If parent is None. Good.

`generate_block_starts`:
```
def generate_block_starts(n,parent,rng):
    starts=[]
    bp=parent_bits_for_n(parent,n)
    if bp is not None:
       if bp.bit_count() == n//2 and n > 0:
          runs,start=bits_to_runs(bp,n)
          if sum(runs) == n:
             starts.append((runs,start))
    props=[0.02,0.04,0.06,0.09,0.12,0.16,0.22,0.30]
    for pr in props:
        mean=int(round(float(n)*pr))
        if mean < 2: mean=2
        min_len=2 if mean >= 6 else 1
        for st in (0,1):
            runs,ss = random_block_runs(n,mean,st,min_len,rng)
            if sum(runs) == n:
                starts.append((runs,ss))
    return starts
```
`bp.bit_count` maybe unavailable? We'll assume. Could define fallback? Could add:
```
def int_bit_count(x): return x.bit_count() if hasattr(int,'bit_count') else bin(x).count('1')
```
But `hasattr(int,'bit_count')` true. To be safe, define:
```
def popcount_int(x):
    try: return x.bit_count()
    except AttributeError: return bin(x).count('1')
```

`binary_search`:
```
def binary_search(n,starts,deadline,rng):
    masks=make_masks_binary(n)
    scored=[]
    for runs,start in starts:
        if not runs: continue
        if sum(runs) != n: continue
        bits=runs_to_bits(runs,start,n)
        if popcount_int(bits) != n//2: continue
        c=binary_cmax_limit(bits,n,masks,1000000000)
        scored.append((c, runs[:], start))
    if not scored:
        return None, 1000000000
    scored.sort()
    best_bits=runs_to_bits(scored[0][1], scored[0][2], n)
    best_c=scored[0][0]
    # refine top candidates up to 5
    max_candidates=min(5,len(scored))
    for idx in range(max_candidates):
        if time.time() > deadline - 3.0: break
        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = min(120.0, remaining * 0.45)
        elif idx == 1:
            alloc = min(70.0, remaining * 0.35)
        else:
            alloc = min(35.0, remaining * 0.20)
        if alloc < 4.0: break
        # If this initial c already worse than best_c + something? Could skip? But lower run count maybe.
        runs, start = scored[idx][1], scored[idx][2]
        b, bc, br, bs = block_refine(runs, start, n, masks, rng, time.time()+alloc)
        if bc < best_c:
            best_c = bc
            best_bits = b
    return best_bits, best_c
```
Sorting tuples with runs lists as second? If equal c, it will try to compare runs lists; lists comparable? Python lists compare lexicographically, okay but maybe large. To avoid, store unique id:

```
scored.append((c, idx, runs[:], start))
scored.sort(key?) no lambda. Tuple with int idx as second avoids compares runs. Use int order.
```

`gradient_polish` as above. Need ensure `pos_idx` not empty. If ntest maybe 0? n>0.

```
def gradient_polish(h,n,rng,deadline):
    h=make_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    alpha=20000.0
    no_imp=time.time()
    while time.time() < deadline:
        g=smooth_jac(h,n,alpha)
        order=np.argsort(g)
        ntest=min(20,n)
        neg_idx=order[:ntest]
        pos_idx=order[n-ntest:][::-1]
        improved=False
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            i=int(pos_idx[int(rng.randint(len(pos_idx)))])
            j=int(neg_idx[int(rng.randint(len(neg_idx)))])
            if i == j: continue
            md = min(float(h[i]), float(1.0 - h[j]))
            if md <= 1e-8: continue
            for frac in (0.5,0.2,0.05,0.01):
                d=md*frac
                nh=h.copy()
                nh[i]-=d; nh[j]+=d
                nh=make_feasible(nh,n)
                c=exact_c5(nh,n)
                if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                    h=nh; best_c=c; best_h=nh.copy(); improved=True; no_imp=time.time(); break
                if time.time() > deadline: break
        if not improved:
            if time.time() - no_imp > 6.0:
                h=best_h.copy()
                no_imp=time.time()
                alpha *= 0.6
                if alpha < 100.0: break
    return best_h, best_c
```
If `pos_idx` length maybe 0? ntest min 20,n, order nonempty. Good. If all g same, pos/neg overlap, i may equal j often; but tries.

```
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent=None; nparent=0; parent_c=1e100

       parent=None; nparent=0
    best_h=None; best_c=1e100; best_n=0
```

```
def parent_array_for_n(parent,n):
   if parent is None: return None
```

Let's include `popcount_int`:
```
def popcount_int(x):
    try:
        return int(x.bit_count())
    except Exception:
        return int(bin(x).count('1'))
```

```
    h=make_feasible(h,n)
    target=n/2.0
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
        # find interior index with value between 1e-6 and 1-1e-6
        idx=np.where((h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9))[0]
        if idx.size:
           h[idx[0]] += diff
           if h[idx[0]]<0 or h[idx[0]]>1:
              # distribute
              ...
        else:
           # adjust last nonboundary
    return h
```

```
       elif h[idx[0]]>1: h[idx[0]]=1
```
This may make sum closer but not exact. If diff small, ok. We'll add in final before return? It could slightly violate bounds if diff > bound; but diff tiny after make_feasible. Good.
Add in `make_feasible` final maybe.

Now, maybe SLSQP returns h with values -1e-8; make_feasible clips.

Now, final code. Let's ensure all helper functions top-level. Good.

Let's produce final. But before final, one more check on gradient with A indexing: We used standard convolution `np.convolve(v,w)`. `np.convolve` full output order: c[k]=∑_i a[i] b[k-i]. Yes. A_r at index n-1+r: ∑_j v[j] w[(n-1+r)-j]. Good.

`np.correlate(w,x)` output order check one more with small actual if possible mentally. Numpy correlate docs: `np.correlate(a,v)` full: c_k = ∑_n a_{n+k} * conj(v_n). For a length M, v length N. The output array is of length M+N-1, and `c` is indexed as `c[k] = ∑ a[n+k] v[n]`, with `k` from -N+1 to M-1. The array starts at k=-N+1. So our out index = k+N-1. Good.
For B_r k=n-1-r, out=2n-2-r. Good. All good.

Now, is the derivative formula using `w` as softmax over c including dx; if dx factor in c, weights unaffected by scalar. Gradient dx*... correct.

Now, maybe SLSQP requires jac not None for all constraints; `sum_con_jac` returns array. Good.

Potential issue: In `sum_con_jac(x,n)`, if x is not array? SLSQP passes array. Return `np.ones(n, dtype=float)`. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def popcount_int(x):
    try:
        return int(x.bit_count())
    except Exception:
        return int(bin(x).count("1"))


def project_sum(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    if abs(float(x.sum()) - target) <= 1e-14:
        return x

    low = -1.0
    high = 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        tmp = x + mid
        np.clip(tmp, 0.0, 1.0, out=tmp)
        s = float(tmp.sum())
        if s < target:
            low = mid
        else:
            high = mid

    x += 0.5 * (low + high)
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    return x


def make_feasible(x, n):
    h = project_sum(x, n)
    target = float(n) / 2.0
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = np.nonzero(h < 1.0 - 1e-12)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            add = diff / float(idx.size)
            max_add = float(np.min(1.0 - h[idx]))
            if add > max_add:
                add = 0.5 * max_add
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = np.nonzero(h > 1e-12)[0]
            if idx.size == 0:
                break
            add = diff / float(idx.size)
            max_add = float(np.min(h[idx]))
            if add < -max_add:
                add = -0.5 * max_add
            if add >= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        idx = np.nonzero((h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9))[0]
        if idx.size:
            h[int(idx[0])] += diff
            np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    return h


def exact_c5(h, n):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))
    return float(np.max(c))


def smooth_value(x, n, alpha):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * dx
    m = float(c.max())
    shifted = alpha * (c - m)
    sm = float(shifted.max())
    vals = np.exp(shifted - sm)
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        s = 1.0
    return float(m + (sm + math.log(s)) / float(alpha))


def smooth_jac(x, n, alpha):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * dx
    m = float(c.max())
    shifted = alpha * (c - m)
    sm = float(shifted.max())
    w = np.exp(shifted - sm)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / s

    v = 1.0 - x
    a = np.convolve(v, w, mode="full")
    start = n - 1
    a = a[start:start + n]

    b = np.correlate(w, x, mode="full")
    if n == 1:
        b = b[0:1]
    else:
        b = b[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]

    return dx * (a - b)


def sum_con_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - float(n) / 2.0)


def sum_con_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def slsqp_optimize(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    x0 = make_feasible(h, n)
    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": sum_con_fun,
            "jac": sum_con_jac,
            "args": (n,),
        }
    ]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_value,
            x0,
            args=(n, alpha),
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=smooth_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-10, "disp": False},
        )
        if res.x is not None:
            return make_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return x0


def optimize_continuous(h0, n, deadline):
    h = make_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)

    if n <= 300:
        passes = [
            (50, 120),
            (500, 150),
            (2500, 150),
            (10000, 120),
        ]
    elif n <= 600:
        passes = [
            (50, 90),
            (500, 100),
            (2500, 100),
            (10000, 90),
        ]
    else:
        passes = [
            (100, 70),
            (1000, 80),
            (5000, 80),
        ]

    for alpha, mi in passes:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        h2 = slsqp_optimize(h, n, alpha, mi)
        c2 = exact_c5(h2, n)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            h = h2

    return best_h, best_c


def make_masks_binary(n):
    masks = [0] * n
    for p in range(1, n):
        masks[p] = (1 << (n - p)) - 1
    return masks


def binary_cmax_limit(bits, n, masks, limit):
    maxc = 0
    h = bits
    for p in range(1, n):
        mask = masks[p]
        c = ((h >> p) & ~h & mask).bit_count()
        if c > maxc:
            if c > limit:
                return c
            maxc = c
        c = (h & ~(h >> p) & mask).bit_count()
        if c > maxc:
            if c > limit:
                return c
            maxc = c
    return maxc


def random_composition(total, parts, min_len, rng):
    if total <= 0:
        return []
    if min_len < 1:
        min_len = 1
    if parts < 1:
        parts = 1
    if parts * min_len > total:
        parts = total // min_len
        if parts <= 0:
            return [total]

    if parts == 1:
        return [total]

    base = [min_len] * parts
    rem = total - parts * min_len
    if rem > 0:
        add = rng.multinomial(rem, parts)
        for i in range(parts):
            base[i] += int(add[i])
    return base


def random_block_runs(n, mean_run, start, min_len, rng):
    half = n // 2
    if half <= 0:
        return [], start

    m = int(round(float(n) / float(max(1, mean_run))))
    if m < 2:
        m = 2
    if m % 2 == 1:
        m += 1
    if m > 2 * half:
        m = 2 * half

    k = m // 2
    len1 = random_composition(half, k, min_len, rng)
    len0 = random_composition(half, k, min_len, rng)
    L = min(len(len1), len(len0))

    runs = []
    if start == 1:
        for i in range(L):
            runs.append(len1[i])
            runs.append(len0[i])
    else:
        for i in range(L):
            runs.append(len0[i])
            runs.append(len1[i])
    return runs, start


def bits_to_runs(bits, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return [], 0
    start = int(bits & 1)
    runs = []
    cur = start
    count = 0
    for i in range(n):
        b = int((bits >> i) & 1)
        if b == cur:
            count += 1
        else:
            runs.append(count)
            cur = b
            count = 1
    runs.append(count)
    return runs, start


def runs_to_bits(runs, start, n):
    bits = 0
    pos = 0
    cur = int(start)
    for L in runs:
        L = int(L)
        if L > 0:
            if cur == 1:
                bits |= ((1 << L) - 1) << pos
            pos += L
            cur ^= 1
    if n > 0:
        bits &= (1 << n) - 1
    return bits


def bits_to_array(bits, n):
    arr = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        arr[i] = 1.0 if int((bits >> i) & 1) else 0.0
    return arr


def continuous_to_bits(arr, n):
    h = np.array(arr, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = n // 2
    if target <= 0:
        return 0
    if target >= n:
        return (1 << n) - 1

    kth = n - target
    if kth < 0:
        kth = 0
    if kth >= n:
        kth = n - 1

    idx = np.argpartition(h, kth)
    idx = idx[kth:]
    bits = 0
    for ii in idx:
        bits |= 1 << int(ii)
    return bits


def is_one_run(i, start):
    if start == 1:
        return (i % 2) == 0
    return (i % 2) == 1


def run_source_lists(runs, start, max_each):
    s1 = []
    s0 = []
    for i in range(len(runs) - 1):
        if runs[i] > 1:
            if is_one_run(i, start):
                s1.append(i)
            else:
                s0.append(i)

    if len(s1) > max_each:
        step = max(1, len(s1) // max_each)
        s1 = s1[::step]
    if len(s0) > max_each:
        step = max(1, len(s0) // max_each)
        s0 = s0[::step]

    return s1, s0


def block_best_improve(runs, start, n, masks, current, time_limit):
    s1, s0 = run_source_lists(runs, start, 30)
    if not s1 or not s0:
        return False, None

    best_c = current
    best_move = None
    lim = current - 1
    if lim < 0:
        lim = 0

    for a in s1:
        for b in s0:
            runs[a] -= 1
            runs[a + 1] += 1
            runs[b] -= 1
            runs[b + 1] += 1

            nb = runs_to_bits(runs, start, n)
            c = binary_cmax_limit(nb, n, masks, lim)

            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_move = (a, b)

            runs[a] += 1
            runs[a + 1] -= 1
            runs[b] += 1
            runs[b + 1] -= 1

            if time.time() > time_limit:
                break
        if time.time() > time_limit:
            break

    if best_move is not None:
        a, b = best_move
        runs[a] -= 1
        runs[a + 1] += 1
        runs[b] -= 1
        runs[b + 1] += 1
        return True, best_c

    return False, None


def block_refine(runs, start, n, masks, rng, deadline):
    runs = list(runs)
    start = int(start)

    if sum(runs) != n:
        bits = runs_to_bits(runs, start, n)
        c = binary_cmax_limit(bits, n, masks, 1000000000)
        return bits, c, runs, start

    bits = runs_to_bits(runs, start, n)
    current = binary_cmax_limit(bits, n, masks, 1000000000)

    best_c = current
    best_bits = bits
    best_runs = list(runs)
    best_start = start

    no_imp = time.time()

    det_dead = min(deadline, time.time() + max(5.0, 0.25 * max(0.0, deadline - time.time())))
    while time.time() < det_dead:
        pass_limit = min(det_dead, time.time() + 3.0)
        ok, nc = block_best_improve(runs, start, n, masks, current, pass_limit)
        if ok:
            current = nc
            bits = runs_to_bits(runs, start, n)
            if current < best_c:
                best_c = current
                best_bits = bits
                best_runs = list(runs)
                best_start = start
            no_imp = time.time()
        else:
            break

    sa_start = time.time()
    sa_duration = max(1e-6, deadline - sa_start)
    T0 = 6.0
    Tend = 0.2

    while time.time() < deadline:
        s1, s0 = run_source_lists(runs, start, 60)
        if not s1 or not s0:
            break

        frac = (time.time() - sa_start) / sa_duration
        if frac < 0.0:
            frac = 0.0
        if frac > 1.0:
            frac = 1.0

        T = T0 * (Tend / T0) ** frac
        if T < Tend:
            T = Tend

        a = int(s1[int(rng.randint(len(s1)))])
        b = int(s0[int(rng.randint(len(s0)))])

        runs[a] -= 1
        runs[a + 1] += 1
        runs[b] -= 1
        runs[b + 1] += 1

        nb = runs_to_bits(runs, start, n)
        lim = current + int(math.ceil(float(T))) + 1
        cand = binary_cmax_limit(nb, n, masks, lim)

        accepted = False
        if cand <= lim:
            delta = cand - current
            if delta <= 0:
                accepted = True
            else:
                if T > 0.0:
                    prob = math.exp(-float(delta) / float(T))
                    if rng.random() < prob:
                        accepted = True

        if accepted:
            current = cand
            bits = nb
            if cand < best_c:
                best_c = cand
                best_bits = bits
                best_runs = list(runs)
                best_start = start
            no_imp = time.time()
        else:
            runs[a] += 1
            runs[a + 1] -= 1
            runs[b] += 1
            runs[b + 1] -= 1

        if time.time() - no_imp > 10.0 and current > best_c + 4:
            runs = list(best_runs)
            start = best_start
            bits = best_bits
            current = best_c
            no_imp = time.time()

    return best_bits, best_c, list(best_runs), best_start


def parent_array_for_n(parent, n):
    if parent is None:
        return None
    if len(parent) == n:
        arr = np.array(parent, dtype=float).copy()
    else:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, len(parent))
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        arr = np.interp(xn, xo, parent)
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    return make_feasible(arr, n)


def parent_bits_for_n(parent, n):
    arr = parent_array_for_n(parent, n)
    if arr is None:
        return None
    return continuous_to_bits(arr, n)


def generate_block_starts(n, parent, rng):
    starts = []

    bp = parent_bits_for_n(parent, n)
    if bp is not None and n > 0:
        if popcount_int(bp) == n // 2:
            runs, start = bits_to_runs(bp, n)
            if sum(runs) == n:
                starts.append((runs, start))

    props = [0.02, 0.04, 0.06, 0.09, 0.12, 0.16, 0.22, 0.30]
    for pr in props:
        mean = int(round(float(n) * float(pr)))
        if mean < 2:
            mean = 2
        min_len = 2 if mean >= 6 else 1
        for st in (0, 1):
            runs, ss = random_block_runs(n, mean, st, min_len, rng)
            if sum(runs) == n:
                starts.append((runs, ss))

    return starts


def binary_search(n, starts, deadline, rng):
    masks = make_masks_binary(n)
    scored = []
    counter = 0

    for runs, start in starts:
        if not runs:
            continue
        if sum(runs) != n:
            continue
        bits = runs_to_bits(runs, start, n)
        if popcount_int(bits) != n // 2:
            continue
        c = binary_cmax_limit(bits, n, masks, 1000000000)
        scored.append((c, counter, list(runs), int(start)))
        counter += 1

    if not scored:
        return None, 1000000000

    scored.sort()
    best_bits = runs_to_bits(scored[0][2], scored[0][3], n)
    best_c = int(scored[0][0])

    max_candidates = min(5, len(scored))
    for idx in range(max_candidates):
        if time.time() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        remaining = deadline - time.time()
        if idx == 0:
            alloc = min(160.0, remaining * 0.55)
        elif idx == 1:
            alloc = min(90.0, remaining * 0.35)
        else:
            alloc = min(45.0, remaining * 0.18)

        if alloc < 4.0:
            break

        runs = scored[idx][2]
        start = scored[idx][3]

        b, bc, br, bs = block_refine(runs, start, n, masks, rng, time.time() + alloc)
        if bc < best_c:
            best_c = bc
            best_bits = b

    return best_bits, best_c


def gradient_polish(h, n, rng, deadline):
    h = make_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    alpha = 20000.0
    no_imp = time.time()

    while time.time() < deadline:
        g = smooth_jac(h, n, alpha)
        order = np.argsort(g)
        ntest = min(20, n)
        neg_idx = order[:ntest]
        pos_idx = order[n - ntest:][::-1]

        improved = False
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break

            i = int(pos_idx[int(rng.randint(len(pos_idx)))])
            j = int(neg_idx[int(rng.randint(len(neg_idx)))])
            if i == j:
                continue

            md = min(float(h[i]), float(1.0 - h[j]))
            if md <= 1e-8:
                continue

            for frac in (0.5, 0.2, 0.05, 0.01):
                d = md * frac
                nh = h.copy()
                nh[i] -= d
                nh[j] += d
                nh = make_feasible(nh, n)
                c = exact_c5(nh, n)
                if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                    h = nh
                    best_c = c
                    best_h = nh.copy()
                    improved = True
                    no_imp = time.time()
                    break
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break

        if not improved:
            if time.time() - no_imp > 6.0:
                h = best_h.copy()
                no_imp = time.time()
                alpha *= 0.6
                if alpha < 100.0:
                    break

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent = None
    nparent = 0
    parent_c = 1e100
    try:
        p = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if p.size > 0:
            nparent = int(p.size)
            p = make_feasible(p, nparent)
            parent_c = exact_c5(p, nparent)
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        nparent = 0

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100
    best_n = 0

    if parent is not None:
        best_h = parent.copy()
        best_c = parent_c
        best_n = nparent
    else:
        n = 250
        runs, ss = random_block_runs(n, 30, 1, 2, rng)
        bits = runs_to_bits(runs, ss, n)
        best_h = bits_to_array(bits, n)
        best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)
        best_n = n

    if parent is not None and time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        alloc = min(80.0, rem * 0.12)
        if alloc >= 15.0:
            hopt, copt = optimize_continuous(parent, nparent, time.time() + alloc)
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h = hopt
                best_c = copt
                best_n = nparent

    if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
        n_list = []
        if (
            parent is not None
            and (nparent % 2 == 0)
            and 128 <= nparent <= 320
        ):
            n_list.append(nparent)
        n_list.append(400)

        unique_n = []
        for nn in n_list:
            if nn not in unique_n:
                unique_n.append(nn)

        binary_total = min(450.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.60)
        if binary_total < 30.0:
            binary_total = 0.0
        binary_deadline = time.time() + binary_total

        for idx, nn in enumerate(unique_n):
            if time.time() > binary_deadline - 10.0:
                break

            remaining_binary = binary_deadline - time.time()
            if len(unique_n) == 1:
                alloc = remaining_binary
            else:
                alloc = remaining_binary * 0.5

            if nn > 350:
                alloc = min(alloc, 220.0)
            else:
                alloc = min(alloc, 150.0)

            alloc = max(15.0, min(alloc, remaining_binary))
            if time.time() + alloc > binary_deadline:
                alloc = binary_deadline - time.time()

            if alloc <= 3.0:
                break

            starts = generate_block_starts(nn, parent, rng)
            b, bc = binary_search(nn, starts, time.time() + alloc, rng)

            if b is not None:
                hbin = bits_to_array(b, nn)
                hbin = make_feasible(hbin, nn)
                cver = exact_c5(hbin, nn)
                cb = float(bc) * (2.0 / float(nn))
                if cb < best_c + 1e-12 and cver < best_c:
                    best_h = hbin
                    best_c = cver
                    best_n = nn

    if (
        parent is not None
        and best_n != 400
        and time.time() < deadline - 80.0
    ):
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        alloc = min(rem * 0.25, 100.0)
        if alloc > 25.0:
            h400 = parent_array_for_n(parent, 400)
            if h400 is not None:
                hopt, copt = optimize_continuous(h400, 400, time.time() + alloc)
                if copt < best_c:
                    best_h = hopt
                    best_c = copt
                    best_n = 400

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        alloc1 = min(rem * 0.5, 180.0)
        if alloc1 > 20.0:
            hopt, copt = optimize_continuous(best_h, best_n, time.time() + alloc1)
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h = hopt
                best_c = copt
                best_n = int(len(best_h))

        scales = [0.02, 0.05, 0.10]
        for sc in scales:
            if time.time() > deadline - 25.0:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            alloc = min(rem * 0.35, 70.0)
            if alloc < 15.0:
                break
            hnoise = best_h + rng.normal(0.0, float(sc), int(best_n))
            hopt, copt = optimize_continuous(hnoise, best_n, time.time() + alloc)
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h = hopt
                best_c = copt
                best_n = int(len(best_h))

    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline - 6.0:
        hpol, cpol = gradient_polish(best_h, best_n, rng, deadline - 2.0)
        if cpol < best_c:
            best_h = hpol
            best_c = cpol
            best_n = int(len(best_h))

    if best_h is None:
        n = 250
        runs, ss = random_block_runs(n, 30, 1, 2, rng)
        bits = runs_to_bits(runs, ss, n)
        best_h = bits_to_array(bits, n)
        best_n = n

    best_h = make_feasible(best_h, int(best_n))
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, int(best_n))

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
